- 24359 nodes · 39666 edges · 2160 communities (1975 shown, 185 thin omitted)

- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness

- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).

## Import Cycles
- 3-file cycle: `src/server/platform/authorization.ts -> src/server/platform/command.ts -> src/server/platform/policy.ts -> src/server/platform/authorization.ts`
- 3-file cycle: `src/server/platform/audit.ts -> src/server/platform/command.ts -> src/server/platform/execution-failure.ts -> src/server/platform/audit.ts`
